- Track P: 560-point grid (q × R × d = 8 × 5 × 14) vs an independent CODATA-2018 oracle (k derived from 1/4πε₀, not copied from the sim) — **0 mismatches**, no NaN/Infinity, `__audit.run()` pass, `__audit.at()` 24 points 0 mismatches. Φ is genuinely computed (`Engine.emf.gaussFlux(q_enc)` with q_enc set by enclosure geometry), not hardcoded: it goes to exactly 0 outside, negative for q < 0, and is invariant under R and under off-centre d < R. Browser-verified.
- Track D report card: V4 C4 R4 L3 G4 **N4** (novice legibility, post-fix; was 2).
- FIXED (9):
  - [P1][FUNC] canvas theme vars were read from `document.documentElement` while the shell's toggle lands `light-theme` on `document.body` — the canvas stayed black under a light chrome and the `.plot-title`/`.legend` overlays flipped to dark text on it. Now reads from `body`, with light-aware grid, "misses"-line and status colours plus a light `.legend` pill.
  - [P1][PHYS] formal row "Field of a point charge (this scene, r = R)" showed E = q/4πε₀R² while the live readout measures the NEAREST wall point, r = R − |d| (d = 0.20 m, R = 0.30 m: readout 4493.78 N/C vs the row's 499.31 N/C). Row relabelled and the KaTeX rewritten to E = |q|/4πε₀(R−|d|)² with the d = 0 limit; the sidebar sub-row now reads "at the nearest wall point".
  - [P1][DEMO] q = 0 nC drew all 24 rays BLUE ("enters the surface" in the legend) while the tally underneath read 0 − 0 = 0 — a zero charge with a drawn field. The ray loop is now skipped at q = 0 and the status line reads "no charge — no field anywhere, so Φ = 0".
  - [P1][DEMO] the "enters AND leaves" line — the entire visual payload of cards 4–5 — was a pale slate indistinguishable from "no crossing" and absent from the legend. Now drawn DASHED and bright, with its own legend entry (so the distinction is not carried by colour alone).
  - [P1][DEMO] world→pixel scale was set by the SHORT axis against a 0.75 m square extent, so R = 0.10 m was a 33 px circle at 1024×768 and the crossing arrowheads were specks. Per-axis fixed extents (0.70 m × 0.55 m) via one shared `pxPerMetre()` used by draw + both drag handlers — same honest single scale at every slider value, ~37 % larger surface. Arrowhead 7 → 9 px.
  - [P1][LAYER] card 1 says "below, watch the two numbers" but the Readouts panel sat under Controls: at 1024×768 both numbers were entirely off the sidebar (flux-val top 821 px vs sidebar bottom 681 px), at 1500×950 "E at the wall" was clipped. Readouts moved above Controls (now 529 px / 614 px, both on screen); the Controls-Guide step order follows.
  - [P1][LAYER] predict-gate leak, card 4: the stem instructed "drag the position slider until the charge sits outside" BEFORE the commit, and that drag reveals Φ = 0.00, the red "charge OUTSIDE" banner and the 3 − 3 = 0 tally.   - [P2][DEMO] canvas tally "crossings drawn: 24 outward − 0 inward = 24" → "field lines at the surface: 24 leaving (+) − 0 entering (−) = net 24"; legend relabelled to "leaves/enters/misses surface"; `Q_enc` glossed as "enclosed charge" on the status line; legend 11 → 12 px.

## 2026-09-11 · `Module-08-Oscillations-and-Waves/C137-the-doppler-effect-sound.html` — jee-sim-deep-review (Track P + Track D incl. D8 + Track L)

- Grid: 27 points browser-read from the live page, re-run at **both** 1500×950 and 1024×768 (54 reads) — v_s {−100, −50, 0, 30, 171, 342, **343**, 344, 450} × v_o {−100, −50, 0, 30, 50, 100} × f₀ {200, 500, 600, 1000} plus 8 cross-combinations of extremes. **0 mismatches** against an independent oracle written from NCERT first principles (f′ = f₀(v±v_o)/(v∓v_s), v = 343 m/s). Shock branch, Mach number, Mach angle and both negative-speed (receding) branches all exact to displayed precision. The JEE-critical asymmetry is implemented correctly and is live-demonstrable: 547.92 Hz (source-toward 30 m/s) vs 543.73 Hz (observer-toward 30 m/s), gap 4.19 Hz.
- Report card: V 4 · C 4 · R 4 · L 3 · G 4 · N 4 (N = D8 novice legibility). Browser-verified: Track P yes · Track D yes (1500×950 and 1024×768, both themes) · Track L yes.
- FIXED [P0][DEMO] the sim's title phenomenon was not drawable. A ±260 m scene holds 300–800 audible wavelengths across ~1150 px, so adjacent crests landed **1.27 px (ahead) / 1.52 px (behind)** apart under a 1.2 px stroke — an axis pixel-scan at the default returned **one contiguous lit run, 189→326 px, zero resolvable circles**; at 1024×768 λ was 0.75 px. Scene rescaled to ±26 m with listeners at ±21 m (:2017) · verified by re-running the same scan: 76 arcs at v_s = 30 with measured gaps 13 px / 15 px against oracle λ_ahead 12.74 px / λ_behind 15.18 px, and 85 arcs at v_s = 200 with 6 px / 22 px against oracle 5.82 px / 22.10 px
- FIXED [P0][DEMO] the drawn sound never reached the listeners. Fronts were retired by COUNT, tying the sound's drawn extent to f₀ (60 fronts = 41 m at 500 Hz but 103 m at 200 Hz) while the listeners sat 615 px outside the outermost wavefront and still displayed a frequency. Retirement is now by radius (`FRONT_MAX_RADIUS = 55 m`, :2113) · verified: wavefronts reach and cross both listeners at every f₀ in range
- FIXED [P0][LAYER] card 3 was false about the UI and prescribed a self-contradicting action — "Two panels on the right show the source-toward and observer-toward readings" (no such panels; the Observed panel shows Ahead/Behind for ONE configuration) and "Slide both to 30 m/s and compare", which measured **595.8 Hz / 500.0 Hz**, neither of the 547.9/543.7 the card claimed. Rewritten to the SME grammar (mechanism told, then v_s = 30 / v_o = 0 → 547.9, then v_s = 0 / v_o = 30 → 543.7) · verified live: both instructions now produce exactly the stated numbers
- FIXED [P1][DEMO] observer motion had no visual at all — v_o moved both readouts while the listeners were drawn static, with no arrow and no label, so half of the sim's own thesis was invisible. Listeners now carry a velocity arrow (toward the source for v_o > 0, flipped for v_o < 0) and a `· vo = N m/s` tag, matching the source's existing idiom; nothing is drawn at v_o = 0 (:2204) · verified at v_o = ±30/±60/±100
- FIXED [P1][FUNC/DEMO] pause-desync: `draw()` was reachable only from `onFrame`/`onResize`, so paused + v_s 30→400 flipped the readout to "no sound yet" while the canvas stayed **byte-identical** (stale arrow, stale "vs = 30 m/s", no Mach cone). All three sliders and `onReset()` now repaint (:2049, :2268) · verified: canvas bytes now change while paused; Reset repaints too (the shell's Reset never calls onResize)
- FIXED [P1][DEMO/D8] every canvas label rendered at 10px. `ctx.font = '600 11px var(--font-sans)'` — canvas cannot resolve `var()`, so the 2D context silently rejected the whole shorthand and kept the spec default `10px sans-serif`; confirmed live (`x.font` unchanged after assignment). Replaced with literal stacks at 12–15px, below-floor sizes raised (:2137) · verified `ctx.font` now reports the real family, all canvas text ≥ 12px
- FIXED [P1][DEMO/D8] light theme canvas illegible: `draw()` read the palette from `document.documentElement` while the toggle sets `body.light-theme`, so `--ink-mid` still resolved to the dark `#cbd5e1`, and the per-listener frequency strings were hard-coded `#e2e8f0` — near-white text on the near-white light surface. Palette now read from `body`, ink from `--ink`, wavefronts switched to a solid blue on light (:2152) · verified `--ink` → `#0f172a` in light
- FIXED [P1][D8] label collision: "Behind" and "Source · vs = 30 m/s" shared a text row and overprinted each other at 1500×950, completely at 1024×768. Fixed row discipline introduced (source name py−34 · listener name py−15 · axis py · v_o arrow py+32 · heard frequency py+48) · verified at both viewports
- FIXED [P2][DEMO/D8] colour contrast: `#06b6d4` and `#a78bfa` sat at ~2.4:1 on the light surface, and they carry the entire ahead/behind distinction. Scene roles moved to `--src` / `--lis-ahead` / `--lis-behind` with light overrides, shared by canvas, legend and readouts so the two can never drift · verified 5.12 / 6.79 / 4.80 on light, 8.02 / 7.15 on dark
- FIXED [P2][D8] ungloss glyphs: the wavefront circles — the primary object — were absent from the legend and never named; the canvas showed a bare `vs/v = 0.09` and bare `547.9 Hz` per listener. Added a ring legend entry "Wavefront — one crest", "Mach number vs/v =", "Emitted f₀ =", and `hears 547.9 Hz`; card 4 now names θ as the cone's half-angle
- ENHANCEMENTS (not applied, ROI-ranked): (1) **λ calipers** — a dimensioned bracket between two adjacent crests ahead and behind, labelled λ′ = (v−v_s)/f₀ and (v+v_s)/f₀ with live metre values, turning the now-visible spacing into a read number; (2) position-aware listener readings so a reading FLIPS as the source passes — the siren-drop moment, currently absent; (3) an f′-vs-time strip synced to the animation, plus an optional audible tone at f′/40; (4) a v_o "ghost" listener that actually translates, so observer motion is motion rather than an arrow; (5) a source/observer A-B toggle that holds one speed fixed and swaps which body carries it (variation theory on the exact JEE contrast).
- Residual (accepted, not defects): for ~2.5 % of the wrap cycle the source re-enters just outside the "Behind" listener, so that listener is briefly in front of the source while still labelled behind — enhancement (2) is the real fix; `EMIT_PERIOD_VISUAL` (:2108) is pre-existing dead code, left untouched for diff minimality; f′ = 171 500 Hz at v_s = 342 is the honest f′→∞ limit and is kept; the only console entry is the server's `/favicon.ico` 404.

Drawn geometry also oracle-checked in pixels: both mass centres within 0.6 px of the computed position at every grid point, and the two series extension arrows measure 108.0/54.0 px vs 108.0/54.0 expected (k₁=100,k₂=200), 277/14 vs 276.6/13.8 at the 20:1 extreme — the drawn x₁:x₂ ratio is k₂:k₁ to 1 %. On-screen period measured by pixel-tracking both blocks at k₁=k₂=100: T_s = 0.89 s, T_p = 0.445 s vs oracle 0.8886 / 0.4443 — the exact factor-2 benchmark. Speed scales `onFrame(dt*speed)` only.   - [P1][DEMO/D8] the two parallel springs were drawn at ±32 px around a 44 px-wide block, so both terminated ~10 px OUTSIDE it: two springs hanging in mid-air above an unattached block, in the column whose entire claim is "both springs share one extension". A rigid yoke is now drawn across the spring ends (`ctx.fillRect(rightCX−pOffset−7, spring_p_Y1, 2*(pOffset+7), 6)`), which is also the physics — the bar is what forces the common displacement that makes stiffnesses add. Mass geometry unchanged (bar hangs from the same `spring_p_Y1`; re-measured centres still within 0.6 px of oracle) · verified by 6× zoom capture
  - [P1][DEMO/D8] light theme was below the contrast floor: every canvas glyph and both sidebar stat groups used the dark-theme pastels `#60a5fa`/`#f472b6`, which measure **2.44:1 / 2.54:1** on the light surface — under 3:1 even for the bold 14px column titles. Added `inks()` + `themeIsLight()` in the sim and `.ink-ser`/`.ink-par`/`.dot-ser`/`.dot-par` in CSS: light theme now takes `#1d4ed8`/`#be185d` (**6.4:1 / 5.8:1**), same two hues, and `drawMass` flips the "1 kg" text to white on the deeper fills · verified computed colours flip on toggle in DOM and canvas (darkest title pixel = rgb(29,78,216))
  - [P1][DEMO/D8] the extension arrows carried bare `x₁ = 0.165 m` / `x = 0.133 m` with an unexplained tick and no statement of what they measure — and `x` collided with the sidebar's `x₀` (displacement from equilibrium). Added a one-line in-scene key on the free band between the plot title and the column titles: "Short tick = that spring's natural (unstretched) length · the arrow below it = how far the spring has stretched" · verified no overflow/collision at 1500×950 and 1024×768 (key 17→628 px, title bottom 26, column titles start at 50)
  - [P1][DEMO/D8] the SERIES/PARALLEL legend pill sat at `top:86px;right:16px` — directly ON the parallel ceiling and its caption at 1024×768 (pill 490–658 px, ceiling 485–705 px). Moved to `bottom:14px` where both columns are clear at every reachable k (lowest pink pixel 477/771 at 1500, 357/550 at 1024, worst case k₁=k₂=400)

  - [P2][LAYER] cards 2, 3 and 4 each posed a question and never answered it ("which mass gets back to the top first?", "which readout moves more?", "why does no such pair exist?") — against the SME grammar's resolve-on-the-spot rule, and a first-encounter student is left holding an unanswered question. Each card gained a terse resolving paragraph with the real numbers (parallel ≈ 2 cycles per series cycle; k_s = 66.7 → 80 while k_p = 300 → 500; k_s = k₁k₂/(k₁+k₂) < both, k_p = k₁+k₂ > both), card 3's UI-narration clause dropped. - Grid: **256 points** driven through `__audit.at` on the live page (|q₁| {0.5, 1.0, 1.7, 3.0} × |q₂| {0.5, 1.0, 1.7, 3.0} × sign(q₂) {−, +} × 8 field points, including both charge-axis extremes, the equatorial line, the frame corner and a point 10⁻³ from the dipole centre), re-run **after** the fixes. **0 mismatches** against an independent Node oracle (E = Σ qᵢ(r−rᵢ)/|r−rᵢ|³ with the sim's 0.02 Plummer softening, plus an independently written arc-length RK4 tracer). Live panel readouts cross-checked at 6 dragged test-point positions, agreeing to the ≤1 px placement quantum (e.g. 0.703 vs 0.707 at (0, 1)). `__audit.run()` `fieldLinesNeverCross` passes; an independent dense segment-intersection sweep over the full traced polylines found **0 genuine crossings** in the dipole, equal-like and 3:1-like configurations (386 dipole intersections are the exact + / − seed-family overdraw, all at ≤5°). Arrowheads verified along E: worst misalignment 4.15° over 161 drawn arrows in four configurations.
- **Line density genuinely encodes the field** (the codebase's uniform-spacing defect class is NOT present): seeds are `round(14·|q|)` per charge, so the drawn line count is proportional to |q| — pixel-counted crossings of a circle around q₁ rise 13 → 15 → 37 → 51 as |q₁| goes 0.5 → 1 → 2 → 3 — and the spacing is traced, not drawn: measured on the sim's own geometry the gap between neighbouring lines grows 0.0915 → 0.7978 world units as r goes 0.20 → 1.60 from the + charge, i.e. ∝ r.
- Report card: V 4 · C 4 · R 3 · L 3 · G 4 · N 4 (N = D8 novice legibility). Browser-verified: Track P yes · Track D yes (1500×950 and 1024×768, both themes) · Track L yes.
- FIXED [P0][FUNC] **"Flip sign" was one-way.** Three consecutive clicks all left `#sign-val` at "+". `#sign-toggle` is an un-shielded `<button>`, so the shell's capture-phase `onParamButton` ran `restartWithCurrentParams()` → `onReset()` (which forces `state.sign2 = −1`) *before* the sim's own handler flipped it — every click produced +1. The dipole could only be recovered with Reset, which also destroyed the inquiry position. Fixed with `cfg.restartOnParamChange:false` (:2934) · verified: four clicks now give + − + −
- FIXED [P0][DEMO] **moving |q₁| or |q₂| silently reset the whole configuration.** Same root cause via `onParamGesture`: with the sign flipped to +, setting q₁ = 2.5 put the sign back to "−" and also discarded any dragged charge positions and the test-point position — the named "changing one control silently resets the configuration" class, and it made the like-charge branch of the sim unreachable in combination with either magnitude slider. Same one-line fix · verified: sign survives a q₁ sweep; the 1024×768 light screenshot now holds q₂ = +1.0 at |q₁| = 3.0 with the null point visible at x ≈ +0.27
- FIXED [P0][PHYS] **card 2's correct answer was false.** It read "No line runs between them — the region between the charges is a gap" with feedback "no line ever enters the region between them. That gap is real." Oracle: for q = (+1, +1) **1493 of 3793 traced polyline points lie in |x| < 0.8, |y| < 1.0** and lines reach x = 0.000 exactly; the live render shows lines filling the whole region. Rewritten to the true topology in SME grammar — no line *joins* two sources, and E = 0 at the **null point** on the axis (midpoint for equal charges; x = −1 + 2√q₁/(√q₁+√q₂) otherwise, oracle-checked at 0.268 for 3:1), around which the lines bend away and thin out. Card 3, the Controls-Guide `#box-sign` step, the canvas step-2 hint and the Info modal's "Serves" line corrected to match
- FIXED [P1][PHYS] **"their density is field strength" asserted without the 2-D caveat** (welcome blurb, Info modal, card 5's "|E| shrinks in step with the spacing"). Measured on the sim's own traced geometry, (1/spacing)/|E| runs **0.470 → 4.028** over r = 0.20 → 1.60 — a factor 8.6 drift, because a flat slice shows spacing ∝ r while |E| ∝ 1/r². NCERT Class 12 Ch. 1 makes exactly this distinction. Card 5 rewritten: the convention is lines per unit *cross-sectional area* ∝ |E|, a statement about a 3-D bundle; on this slice the gaps widen ∝ r while |E| falls as 1/r², so crowded still means strong but the two do not fall at the same rate. Physics depth raised, not simplified
- FIXED [P1][DEMO/D8] **light-theme legend at ~2.2:1 contrast.** `.legend` pins `background:rgba(6,14,17,.72)` while `color:var(--text)` flips to `#0f172a` in light theme — measured `rgb(15,23,42)` on that near-black pill, i.e. the sim's ONLY glyph key was dark-on-dark. Light-theme override added (`rgba(248,250,252,.88)` / `#0f172a`, ≈17:1) · verified by computed style in both themes
- FIXED [P1][DEMO/D8] **the key named the wrong glyphs.** The legend listed only "+q / −q / test" — the blue curves (the sim's entire subject) and the yellow E arrow had no entry at all, while "test" was shown from boot although the test point only appears at card 4, and "−q" stayed on screen describing nothing once q₂ was flipped positive. Legend now reads "positive charge +q · negative charge −q · field line (tangent to E) · test point & its E arrow", raised from 11px to 12px, with the −q and test rows staged by `state.sign2` / `state.showTest` from `draw()`; the plot title now states the grid scale ("grid square = 1 unit") · verified: test row `legend-off` at boot, live at card 4; −q row drops when both charges are +
- FIXED [P1][D8] **charge labels were struck through.** `q₁ = +1.0` / `q₂ = −1.0` were drawn as bare canvas text over the x-axis and the near-horizontal field lines leaving each charge, illegible at 1024×768 in both themes. Labels now sit on a background chip, reusing the |E| chip idiom already in `draw()` · verified at both viewports, both themes
- FIXED [P2][PHYS] **the test point could be dragged onto a charge, where the readout was finite and wrong.** Inside the 0.02 softening |E| stops obeying 1/r²: it peaks at 962 near r = 0.014 and then *collapses to 0.250* sitting exactly on the charge, where the field diverges. The probe is now held at r ≥ 0.16 from either charge (≥ the 14 px drawn disc at every supported viewport) · verified: dragging the test point onto q₁ now reads **|E| = 38.074**, inside the oracle's 37.95–38.46 band for that shell
- ENHANCEMENTS (not applied, ROI-ranked): (1) **draw the null point** — an open marker with an "E = 0" tag at x = −1 + 2√q₁/(√q₁+√q₂) whenever both charges are like-signed, plus a faint separatrix; the concept card 2 now teaches is currently inferable but not drawn, and the marker would move live as |q₁|/|q₂| change, which is the JEE null-point question made visual; (2) a **single-charge regime** — extend |q₂| down to 0 (rendered as a hollow "q₂ = 0" marker) or add a preset row {single +, dipole, like pair, 3:1 unequal}, since the canonical isolated-point-charge picture is unreachable in the present 0.5–3 range; (3) an **equipotential overlay** toggle — `Engine.em.equipotentialAt` is already in the bundled engine and unused, and the ⊥ relationship between V-contours and field lines is the highest-value linkage this topic can show; (4) a **dipole/like ghost overlay** so the flip is an A-B comparison rather than a memory test; (5) a separation slider (d between the charges) — reachable today only by dragging, and never announced as a parameter.
- Residual (accepted, not defects): the drawn + and − seed families coincide exactly for the symmetric dipole, so 28 polylines render 14 distinct curves (harmless overdraw, 0 crossings); the 0.02 softening still biases |E| ~2% low at the new r = 0.16 clamp; the deck carries one gated question rather than the SME's 3–5 (restructuring it is a deck rewrite, not a surgical fix); the only sim-side console entry is the static server's `/favicon.ico` 404.
- Gates: syntax ✓ (7/7 inline scripts) · jsdom ×3 ✓ (welcome-overlay, controls-tutorial 9 steps, guided-inquiry 5 cards) · user-version-eval ✓ · layout-probe ✓ · canvas-check ✓ (`#scene` backing:display 1.000/1.000 at 1500×950 and 1024×768, identical to the pre-fix baseline via `git show HEAD:…`) · copyright ✓

## 2026-09-11 · `Module-08-Oscillations-and-Waves/C135-standing-waves-in-air-columns-open-and-closed-pipes.html` — jee-sim-deep-review (Track P + Track D incl. D8 + Track L)

- Grid: **30 points** (L ∈ {0.20, 0.35, 0.50, 0.70, 0.85, 1.20} m × n ∈ {1…5}, i.e. both slider extremes, the default, and every odd/even harmonic including all four "forbidden" even-n closed-pipe cases), read from the live page (`#f-open`, `#f-closed`, `#lam-open`, `#lam-closed`, `window.__audit.at`) and re-run **after** the fixes. **0 mismatches** against an independent Node oracle (f_open = n·343/(2L), λ_open = 2L/n; f_closed = n·343/(4L) for odd n only, λ_closed = 4L/n; "no mode" for even n). `__audit.run()` `closedPipeHasNoEvenHarmonics` passes. Drawn node/antinode patterns verified against the boundary conditions at n = 1…5 in both views: open pipe = displacement antinode at both ends (n+1 A, n N); closed pipe = antinode at the open top, node at the stopped bottom, (n+1)/2 each; pressure view = the exact spatial derivative (sin where displacement is cos), so a pressure NODE sits at the open end and a pressure ANTINODE at the closed end. Speed control verified as playback-only: measured on-screen period 2.05 / 1.03 / 4.12 s at 1× / 2× / 0.5× against a computed 2.04 / 1.02 / 4.08 s.
- Report card: V 4 · C 4 · R 3 · L 4 · G 4 · N 4 (N = D8 novice legibility). Browser-verified: Track P yes · Track D yes (1500×950 and 1024×768, both themes) · Track L yes.
- FIXED [P0][DEMO] **the standing wave never oscillated — it aliased.** `timePhase = cos(2π·f·state.t·0.005)` with `state.t` in *milliseconds* put the open pipe on screen at **1713 Hz** and the closed pipe at 857 Hz (measured: state.t advances 0.999 units per ms of wall clock), i.e. ~28.6 and ~14.3 cycles *per frame* at 60 fps. Sampled per-frame phase was `[0.635, −0.773, 0, −0.194, −0.635, 1, −0.635, …]` — incoherent noise — and the mid-row curve x-position jittered 698→736→703→722 px with no periodicity. The sim's two headline observables (the air column breathing; the closed pipe swinging at *half* the open pipe's rate) were therefore not on screen at all. Replaced with one declared slow-motion factor applied identically to both pipes, `cos(2π·(f/SLOWMO)·(t/1000))`, `SLOWMO = 700` (:2032, :2218) · verified: max |Δphase| per frame now **0.043** (was ~1.0), phase series monotone `[0.999, 0.996, 0.991, 0.982, 0.971, …]`, on-screen 0.490 Hz open vs 0.245 Hz closed — exactly 2 : 1
- FIXED [P0][DEMO] **the pipe-length slider was pixel-dead.** `pipeH = availH * 0.88` at every L, so the drawn geometry was byte-identical at L = 0.20, 0.50 and 1.20 m (measured pipe span 683 px at all three) — only the ruler caption and the numbers moved. The named auto-fit-cancels-the-dependence class. Replaced with one honest metres→pixels scale fixed by the slider maximum (`pxPerM = availH·0.88 / L_MAX_M`, top-aligned) (:2380–2397) · verified: measured wall length is now exactly linear in L — 101 / 189 / 276 / 364 / 453 / 540 px at L = 0.20 / 0.40 / 0.60 / 0.80 / 1.00 / 1.20 (fit 438.75·L + 13.5, R ≈ 1)
- FIXED [P0][LAYER] **card 3's predict gate leaked its own answer.** `onStep(2)` set n = 2 as the card opened, so the canvas already read "no n = 2 / in a stopped pipe / (next mode: n = 3)" and the sidebar "no mode" while the student was still being asked "What happens on the closed pipe?" — with choice C reading "There isn't one — its next mode is n = 3". Staged: the card now holds n = 1 and a sim-side `.choice` listener applies the reveal only on commit (`REVEAL_ON_COMMIT`, :2467–2505) · verified live: pre-commit `{step:2, n:1, fClosed:"171.5 Hz"}`, post-commit `{n:2, fClosed:"no mode", answered:true}`, and revisiting the card after answering keeps it revealed
- FIXED [P1][FUNC] **canvas kept dark-theme colours in light mode.** `getCss()` read the custom properties off `document.documentElement` while the theme toggle lands `light-theme` on `<body>`; measured in light theme `--muted` = `#334155` on body but `getCss('--muted')` returned `#cbd5e1`. Every canvas glyph — pipe walls, sub-labels, N/A letters, the L ruler, the reading key — rendered near-white on the near-white light plot box. Lookup order reversed to body-first (:2367) · verified: `getCss('--muted')` → `#334155`, `getCss('--text')` → `#0f172a` under `body.light-theme`
- FIXED [P1][FUNC] **pause-desync, and a View toggle that repainted the wrong scene.** The canvas was repainted only by `onFrame`, so while paused a slider change updated the readouts and left the picture stale (hash byte-identical across L 0.50→1.20, n 1→5). Worse, `#view-disp/#view-pres` carried no `data-no-reset`, so the shell's capture-phase `onParamButton` ran `restartWithCurrentParams()` → `onReset()` → `draw()` with the DEFAULTS and stopped there: paused, clicking Pressure at L = 1.20, n = 5 painted the L = 0.50, n = 1 displacement scene while `state` said pressure/1.20/5. Added `draw()` to the length, harmonic and both View listeners (:2091–2118) and `data-no-reset` on `#box-view` (:1198) · verified: paused slider and View changes now repaint, and the View click no longer zeroes the run
- FIXED [P1][D8] **the legend covered the pipe captions at 1024×768.** The fixed `topMargin = 80` ignored the legend pill (measured bottom 73 px into the canvas) and "CLOSED PIPE" was drawn at y ≈ 64–78. `topMargin` is now measured from the legend's own rect (:2371–2378) · verified by screenshot at both viewports
- FIXED [P1][D8] **λ was claimed but never shown, and v was invisible.** The Info modal's K3 row asserted "Nodes (N), antinodes (A), λ, f are all drawn on the pipes" — λ appeared nowhere, and the 343 m/s behind every displayed frequency was in no readout, so f = nv/2L could not be checked against anything on screen. Added `λ = …` under each pipe on the canvas, a `Open λ = 2L/n` / `Closed λ = 4L/n` / `Sound speed v` block to the sidebar panel (renamed "Wavelength and frequency"), and the v + slow-motion disclosure to the canvas key; K3 and the Controls-Guide step reworded to match (:1210–1224, :2112–2130, :2318, :2400–2410) · verified across the 30-point grid, e.g. L = 0.50 n = 3 → open λ 0.33 m / 1029.0 Hz, closed λ 0.67 m / 514.5 Hz
- FIXED [P1][D8] **nothing said the sideways bulge means motion ALONG the pipe.** The only gloss was an 11px "View: displacement (N = displacement node, A = antinode)" line — the classic transverse-vs-longitudinal and displacement-vs-pressure confusion left entirely to the reader. Replaced with a three-line canvas key that names the encoding in words and re-words itself per view ("Sideways width = how far the air moves back and forth ALONG the pipe" / "… how far the pressure swings above and below normal"), plus per-view N/A glosses ("air stays still" / "no pressure swing") (:2400–2412)
- FIXED [P2][D8] **canvas text below the 12px floor, and colour as the only channel.** Pipe sub-labels, the N/A letters, the n caption and the next-mode hint were 11px; the legend 11px. Raised to 12.5–13px; the N/A letters now draw in `--text` instead of the pale `#fef3c7` (unreadable on the light plot box) and antinode dots are drawn larger than node dots, so node-vs-antinode survives red-green colour blindness on three channels (letter, size, colour) (:2301–2317, :2334–2346, :118)
- FIXED [P2][LAYER] **card copy referenced actions the shell performs, in the retired instruction style.** "Set the harmonic to n = 2" (done by `onStep`/the commit reveal), "Drag the pipe length", "Sweep n through 1, 2, 3, 4, 5" removed; card 3 restated as a neutral claim question ("A pipe open at both ends has a second harmonic, n = 2… Does a pipe stopped at one end have one too?") with terse claim-only choices and feedback that resolves on the spot, per the SME grammar in `guided-inquiry/references/golden-flows.md`. 4 cards, all gates intact
- ENHANCEMENTS (not applied, ROI-ranked): (1) **harmonic-spectrum ladder** in the (now honestly) empty lower canvas — two vertical frequency axes sharing one scale, open-pipe ticks at f₁, 2f₁, 3f₁… and closed-pipe ticks at f₁, 3f₁, 5f₁… with the missing even rungs drawn as greyed ghosts and the current n highlighted; sweeping n would walk both ladders together, making "skips every even one" a single seen picture instead of five remembered frames, and it is the one representation the topic needs that the sim has no form of; (2) a **speed-of-sound / temperature control** (v = 331 + 0.6·T, or a gas selector He/air/CO₂) — v is currently a hidden constant, so the third variable in f = v/λ can never be varied and the resonance-tube regime is unreachable; (3) an **audible tone** per pipe (WebAudio, gated behind a speaker button) — this is the one JEE topic whose observable is literally a sound, and the octave-vs-twelfth distinction is instantaneous by ear; (4) a **longitudinal particle strip** beside each pipe (a row of air dots that actually compress and rarefy along the axis) to close the transverse-rendering gap that the new key currently closes only in words; (5) an **end-correction toggle** (L_eff = L + 0.6r per open end) — a standard JEE Advanced refinement the sim silently omits.
- Residual (accepted, not defects): at short L a large part of the canvas below the pipes is empty — that is the honest consequence of the fixed metres→pixels scale, and it is exactly the band enhancement (1) would occupy; the bundled engine's `normalMode` reports `nodeCount = n − 1` for `'both-free'` (should be n; a mode with n+1 antinodes must have n nodes between them) — wrong, but never read by this sim, and the block is the shared kernel guarded by `kernel-diff`, so it is reported rather than patched here; the only console entry is the static server's `/favicon.ico` 404.
- Gates: syntax ✓ (7/7 inline scripts) · jsdom ×3 ✓ (welcome-overlay, controls-tutorial 10 steps, guided-inquiry 4 cards, all `--baseline`) · user-version-eval ✓ · layout-probe ✓ · canvas-check ✓ (`#scene` backing:display 1.000/1.000, drift 0.00% vs the pre-fix baseline from `git show HEAD:…`) · copyright ✓
- S3: re-push required for this file.

## 2026-09-11 · `Module-09-Electrostatics/C154-dielectrics-polarisation-and-capacitance-change.html` — jee-sim-deep-review (Track P + Track D incl. D8 + Track L)

- Track P: grid 65 points (x ∈ {0, .25, .5, .75, 1} × κ ∈ {1, 1.1, 2, 4, 5.5, 8} × {battery connected, disconnected}, plus a 5-point frozen-charge branch disconnected at x = 0.5), **0 mismatches** against an independent oracle (C = C₀[1 + (κ−1)x] for the side-by-side parallel combination; C₀ = ε₀A/d = 88.5419 pF, Q₀ = 1.06250 nC). `__audit.at` reproduces the oracle to double precision at both battery states; the `chargeFrozenWhenBatteryDisconnected` invariant passes at 0 exactly. Browser-verified yes (CDP :12226, 1500×950 and 1024×768, both themes).
- Track D report card: V 3 → 4 · C 3 → 4 · R 3 · L 3 → 4 · G 4 · **N (D8) 2 → 4**
- FIXED [P1][PHYS] **the drawn bound charge was scaled by the wrong denominator, so the polarisation picture did not account for the field it draws.** `nPol` used `nDraw × (slabW/capW) × (1−1/κ)` — the slab's share of the *whole plate area* — where σ_bound/σ_free(slab) = (κ−1)/κ applies to the free charge *over the slab*, i.e. `nSlabSym`. Live draw-call capture at x = 0.5, κ = 4, battery connected: 11 bound glyphs drawn where 18 is correct (nSlabSym = 24 × 0.75); at x = 0.1, κ = 8: 3 drawn (the `Math.max(3, …)` floor) where 8 is correct. The net drawn density over the slab came out at 2.17 u against 1 u in the vacuum region while the field arrows — correctly — had equal length in both, so the charge picture contradicted the field picture at every partial insertion. Only x = 1 happened to agree. Now `Math.round(nSlabSym * polMag)` with the floor dropped so the layer fades to nothing as κ → 1 (:2501) · verified by draw-call capture: x = .5 κ = 4 → 18, x = .5 κ = 8 → 42, x = .1 κ = 8 → 8, x = .5 κ = 1.1 → 1, x = .5 κ = 1 → 0, x = 1 unchanged
- FIXED [P1][DEMO] **the "not to scale" disclosure was covered by the legend.** `.plot-title` and `.legend` were separately absolute at the same corner; measured at 1024×768 the opaque pill overlapped the title by 76 px, hiding exactly "· not to scale". Both now live in one wrapping flex row `.plot-head` (:109–120, :1305–1313) · verified: title rect 31→407 fully clear, legend drops to its own row at 1024
- FIXED [P1][DEMO/D8] **the legend was unreadable in light theme.** The pill kept a hardcoded `rgba(6,14,17,.72)` fill while `color:var(--text)` flips to near-black — sampled from the rendered PNG, text `rgb(15,23,42)` on composited `rgb(73,79,82)` = **2.15:1**. The only key to the sim's colour code failed in one of the two shipped themes. Added the light-theme pill variant (:118) · verified: `rgba(255,255,255,.88)` under `body.light-theme`
- FIXED [P1][DEMO/D8] **the induced surface charges — the mechanism the topic is named after — were unlabelled and colour-coded almost identically to the free plate charges** (bound + `#fde68a` vs plate + `#fbbf24`; bound − `#93c5fd` vs plate − `#60a5fa`), and absent from the legend. Added in-slab `induced −` / `induced +` labels with a knockout so they survive over the field arrows, drawn only when the slab is wide enough to hold them (:2503–2520)
- FIXED [P1][DEMO/D8] **the field arrows were never identified.** Fourteen vertical arrows whose *length* carries E, with no glyph name, no legend entry and no statement of the encoding. Added an `electric field` / `length ∝ E` label to the left of the plates, vertically centred on the gap and width-guarded (:2540–2550)
- FIXED [P1][DEMO] **capacitance — the entire subject of the sim — was the least salient thing on screen.** C was 12 px muted grey on the last text baseline of the canvas (y = 765 of 771), ~530 px below the capacitor, and the Readouts panel carried only Q and V, so nothing on screen let a student check "C rises in both cases", which cards 2, 3 and 5 all assert. Moved the canvas line to sit directly under the plates as `capacitance C = 221.4 pF = 2.50 × C₀` in 13 px `--text` (:2700–2712) and added a `C (capacitance)` row above Q and V carrying the same ×C₀ ratio (:1417, :2296, :2342) — the ratio is the number the JEE questions actually turn on
- FIXED [P2][DEMO] **the gap never grew with the viewport.** `capH` was hard-capped at 140 px, so the slab, the arrows and both induced layers stayed fixed-size while the lower half of a 771 px canvas sat empty. Now `Math.min(h*0.24, 180)`, which leaves the 1024×768 case (h = 589 → 141 px) untouched and gains 29 % on tall canvases; `cyTop` floored at 138 px so the Q callout clears the header when the legend wraps (:2383–2391) · verified by screenshot at both viewports — the wrapped legend had briefly covered the Q callout at 1024 and this floor is the fix
- FIXED [P2][D8] **sub-12px canvas text and a struck-through label.** Voltmeter scale labels 10 → 12 px (and `#94a3b8` → `#cbd5e1`); `.legend-item` and `.plot-inline` 11 → 12 px; the bold-12px "disconnected" caption moved from `wireY + 30` to `wireY − 24`, where the return wire at `battX − 40` / `wireY + 40` no longer runs through it (:2634, :114–117, :2587)
- FIXED [P2][LAYER] **card copy carried UI choreography and used C before naming it.** "Then press Next", "(see Controls)", "(Connected in Controls)" removed from cards 1, 2 and 4; card 1's choreography paragraph replaced with the two definitions the flow silently assumed — capacitance (C₀ = Q₀/V₀ = 88.5 pF, "the charge it stores per volt") and dielectric — per the SME grammar in `guided-inquiry/references/golden-flows.md`. The gate, the three claim-only choices and the resolve-on-the-spot feedback were already conformant and are untouched. Controls-Guide Readouts step reworded to match the new C row (:1225, :1330, :1346, :3067)
- ENHANCEMENTS (not applied, ROI-ranked): (1) **an energy row plus a before/after ratio strip** — `computeState` already returns `energyMicroJoules` and nothing displays it, so the half of the battery-on/battery-off contrast that JEE examines hardest (U rises ×κ with the battery on, *falls* /κ when isolated, and the slab is pulled in either way) is currently unreachable; a `U` readout plus a four-bar C/Q/V/U strip showing each quantity against its slab-out value would make both columns of the standard exam table visible at once instead of remembered across a switch flip; (2) **molecular dipoles inside the slab** — the sim draws the *result* of polarisation (two surface layers) but never the mechanism, so the causal chain E₀ → dipoles align → bulk cancels → only the faces are left is asserted in card text and shown nowhere; a sparse grid of small ⊖–⊕ pairs that rotate/stretch with κ, fading as κ → 1, is the one representation this topic needs and has no form of; (3) **replace the free-charge text glyphs with a proportional tick/bar layer** — at κ = 8, x = 1 the top row draws 96 bold-14px `+` across 554 px (5.8 px pitch, a solid bar), so the κ-fold density contrast the encoding exists to show saturates exactly where it should be most dramatic; (4) **a partial-thickness slab mode** (t < d, full area) alongside the present side-by-side geometry — that is the C = ε₀A/(d − t + t/κ) case and the only one in which E actually drops to E₀/κ inside the dielectric, which is what most students expect to see here and currently cannot; (5) **a κ → ∞ conducting-slab preset** to close the series.
- Residual (accepted, not defects): the field arrows are drawn the same length inside the slab and in the vacuum gap — *correct* here, since the two regions are capacitors in parallel at the same V and the same d, so E = V/d in both (verified against the oracle; the `electric field / length ∝ E` label and the now-consistent charge layers are what let a student read this rather than mistake it for a bug); at the gated card 2 the Insertion slider stays live, so the prediction is bypassable by experiment — nothing on screen leaks the answer pre-commit, and gating the control would need a renderer/staging hook rather than a copy change; the only console entry at any grid point is the static server's `/favicon.ico` 404.

Signs verified both ways — q > 0 ⇒ dV/dr < 0 ⇒ E_r > 0 outward; q < 0 ⇒ dV/dr > 0 ⇒ E_r < 0 inward, i.e. **downhill in V in both cases**. The central difference (h = 1e−5) carries a relative error of h²/r² ≤ 1.1e−9, so card 2's "agree to four decimal places at every position, every charge" is literally true across the grid; `__audit.invariants.gradientEqualsFieldEverywhere` → max relative deviation 6.26e−10. - ENHANCEMENTS (not applied, ROI-ranked): (1) **a regime selector — uniform field (parallel plates) and two-charge presets beside the present point charge.** The sim demonstrates exactly one V(r), the 1/r of a point charge, so three of the four regimes JEE actually examines under E = −dV/dx are unreachable: the *linear* V of a parallel-plate gap where E = V/d is constant (the most-asked application, and the only case where "slope = −E" can be read straight off a straight line); a V minimum between two like charges, where **E = 0 while V ≠ 0**; and the midpoint of a dipole, where **V = 0 while E ≠ 0** — the single most-tested confusion in the topic, which the sim currently cannot produce at any slider setting because V and |E| are strictly monotone together in q/r. - FIXED [P0][FUNC] **moving the contour-level slider silently wiped the configuration, and "Reset work counter" reset the whole sim.** `cfg.restartOnParamChange` defaults to true; on any parameter gesture the shell calls `onReset()` and then restores only controls that carry a *value*. The q₂ sign is set by two `<button>`s, so it had nothing to restore — and the test-charge sliders are `data-no-reset`, so `onReset()` moved them to the default and the shell left them there. Live trace: `{sign:+1, tx:−2.00, ty:0.80, work:−0.006}` → move level slider → `{sign:−1, tx:0.00, ty:1.50, work:0.000}`; pressing *Reset work counter* did the same. A timeless slider-driven diagram has no run to restart, so the documented wholesale opt-out now applies: `cfg:{timeless:true, restartOnParamChange:false}` (:3277) · verified: same trace now holds sign, position and work across both gestures, and *Reset work counter* zeroes only the counter
- FIXED [P0][PHYS/DEMO] **the headline "crossing angle" contradicted the sim's own claim exactly where the cards told the student to look.** `measureAtSample` took the contour tangent from the nearest *marching-squares segment midpoint* — a different point, up to 0.3 world units away, on a 0.05 grid — and compared it with E at the test point. Walking the V = 0.50 dipole contour the readout gave **89.7°, 89.9°, 88.6°, 89.6°, 89.3°**, and at (0, 0.40) it gave **75.8°**, while cards 2 and 4 and the Info modal all assert "the measured angle stays at 90.0° everywhere". Off the contour it fell back to the analytic perpendicular of E and printed 90.0° *by construction* — so it read exactly 90° where nothing was measured and < 90° where it was. The tangent now comes from a central-difference gradient of the **potential** (a scalar sum) tested against the **field** (an independent vector sum) at the *same* point: the 90.0° is a real numerical check of E = −∇V, not an identity (:2698–2724) · verified: 90.0° at all 96 grid points, oracle worst |90 − θ| = 7.1e-7°; the drawn cyan tangent and the green right-angle marker are now genuinely square
- FIXED [P1][DEMO] **the faint context contours were spaced at unequal ΔV, so "crowded = strong field" read backwards.** Levels were ±{0.2, 0.4, 0.7, 1, 1.5, 2} — ΔV of 0.2, 0.3 and 0.5 mixed. Oracle along +x from the midpoint: drawn gaps 0.083 → 0.121 → 0.099 → 0.123 → 0.094 while |E| fell *monotonically* 5.99 → 4.09 → 3.02 → 2.43 → 2.13, i.e. the single most-taught reading of a contour map came out wrong three times out of four. Now a constant `CONTOUR_STEP = 0.25` (:2589, :2677–2689), for which the gap is exactly ΔV/|E| — same sweep gives strictly increasing 0.038 → 0.045 → 0.055 → 0.066 → 0.081 → 0.097 → 0.113. Alpha 0.18 → 0.34 and an on-canvas gloss "Faint rings: equal ΔV = 0.25 steps — crowded = strong E" · verified by oracle and screenshot; 81-step slider sweep costs 3 ms/step with the 17 levels
- FIXED [P1][FUNC/D8] **every canvas label rendered at 10 px.** Four `ctx.font` assignments used `var(--font-sans)` / `var(--font-nums)`; canvas cannot parse `var()`, so the whole declaration is rejected and the context keeps `10px sans-serif` — confirmed live (`c.font='bold 18px var(--font-sans)'` → `'10px sans-serif'`). The charge +/− glyphs, the q₁/q₂ labels, the midpoint V and |E| chip and the angle readout were all affected. Literal stacks now, and every size ≥ 12 px (:2728–2732, and the four call sites)
- FIXED [P1][FUNC/D8] **the sim's headline number rendered as three stacked characters.** `.stat-label{flex-shrink:0}` starved the value of width: `#angleVal` measured **28 × 86 px — "90" / ".0" / "°" on three lines** at both 1500×950 and 1024×768. The label now wraps and the value stays on one line (:157–163) · verified: 54 × 29 px, one line, at both viewports
- FIXED [P1][FUNC/D8] **the light theme shipped a dark canvas with dark-on-dark text over it.** `css()` read the theme vars off `document.documentElement` while the shell puts `light-theme` on `document.body` (:2222) — measured: `documentElement --bg` = `#060E11` vs `body --bg` = `#e2e8f0`, canvas pixel unchanged at `[6,14,17]` after the toggle. The legend pill and plot title, which sit *on* the canvas, meanwhile flipped their ink to near-black over their hardcoded dark fill and vanished. C023) covering all eleven drawn colours, plus light-theme rules for `.legend` / `.plot-title` (:123–128, :2733–2754) · verified: canvas pixel `[248,250,252]` after the toggle, legend `rgb(15,23,42)` on `rgba(248,250,252,.86)`, screenshots legible in both themes at both viewports
- FIXED [P1][DEMO] **62 % of the contour-level slider drew nothing, silently.** With like charges V > 0 everywhere in the frame (minimum 0.477 at a corner), so every level from −2.00 to +0.45 — 50 of the 81 slider positions — produced **0 contour segments** and the cyan curve simply disappeared with no explanation. - FIXED [P1][LAYER] **card 3's gated prediction had its answer printed on the canvas, and instructed a toggle that was already set.** The card asks "V is zero but E is large?" while `V(0,0) = 0.00` / `|E(0,0)| = 2.00` and the midpoint E arrow were on screen from boot. The numbers now render as `= ?` and the arrow is withheld until the prediction is committed (`midpointAnswerRevealed`, keyed off the shell's `data-ready` stamp, and always revealed outside guided inquiry) (:2906–2919, :3172–3180); `onStep(2)` guarantees the dipole so the copy no longer has to ask for it. - FIXED [P1][DEMO/D8] **the misconception-busting midpoint arrow was amber drawn on top of an amber field line, and unlabelled.** Card 3's whole payload is "E is large here", carried by a 2.5 px arrow indistinguishable from the axial field line beneath it. - FIXED [P2][D8] **unnamed glyphs and unstated normalisation.** The orange and cyan sticks at the test charge are now labelled "E" and "along V" in the scene and the tangent is dashed so the two are told apart without colour; the legend gained word-glosses ("Field line — direction of E", "Equipotential — V is constant along it", "Test charge (drag me)"); a bottom-left note states the natural units (k = 1, |q| = 1) that every number on screen silently assumed; the readout labels became "Angle between E and the contour" and "Work you do, W = q ΔV" with the q = +1 convention spelled out (:1510–1512, :1632–1639, :2850–2864, :3023–3032)

It now tests the actual claim — gradient-of-V tangent against the field, at the same point, over 242 grid points spanning both configurations — at a real `tol = 1e-6` (:3221–3256) · verified: worst |cos| = 1.4e-8, pass
- ENHANCEMENTS (not applied, ROI-ranked): (1) **a potential-landscape layer** — a diverging heat map of V under the contours (plus a colour bar, and optionally a small 3-D "hill" inset). The topic's actual object is a scalar field and the sim draws only its level sets, so "an equipotential is a contour line on a hill" is asserted in the cards and shown nowhere; `rebuildContours` already samples V on a 120×120 grid, so this is a `putImageData` of that array through a fixed ±2 ramp, drawn behind the grid, revealed at card 4 — it would make the crowding, the V = 0 plane and the like-charge saddle all readable in one glance; (2) **magnitude sliders for q₁ and q₂, not just a sign toggle** — unequal charges is the JEE staple the sim cannot pose: for +q and −2q the V = 0 surface is a *sphere* (Apollonius), not the plane the sim always shows, and `fieldLineSeeds` already scales seed count by |q|; (3) **a W-versus-path-length strip chart** — "zero work along an equipotential" is currently a counter that fails to move, which is a non-event; a trace that runs flat along a contour and steps across it turns the null result into something seen; (4) **single-charge and uniform-field presets** — concentric circles and parallel straight lines are the two canonical equipotential pictures every textbook opens with and neither is reachable here; (5) **a conductor preset** (a metal slab in the field, its surface an equipotential with E ⊥ to it) to close the loop to the conductors chapter.
- Residual (accepted, not defects): the legend pill at the top-right can be occluded by the test charge dragged into the extreme corner (|x|, |y| ≳ 2.6) — it is `pointer-events:none` so the drag is unaffected, and relocating it to the bottom-right would collide with the fixed Controls-Guide nav; `__audit.at` still reports `fieldTangentDotProduct` from a marching-squares segment tangent (3.1e-5 at the default inputs), kept as an honest mesh-quality number now that the perpendicularity *claim* is carried by the gradient invariant; the only console entry at any grid point is the static server's `/favicon.ico` 404.

Measured consecutive-frame peak spans: at the **default 30 Hz** the string was frozen (67, 78, 76, 75, 74 px out of a 311 px envelope — 30 Hz is exactly Nyquist, aliased to ≈DC, so pressing Play on defaults showed a motionless arch); at **f₁ = 50 Hz**, the card-2 answer, the frames cycled 51/173/123/51/173/123 — a three-frame strobe, not an oscillation; at 250 Hz the string went flat (span 0) every third frame. Added `TIME_SCALE = 1/40` on the animation clock only — every frequency, speed and readout stays the true SI value — disclosed on screen in the plot title as "motion shown in slow motion, 1/40 of real speed" (:2037, :2450–2456, :1106) · re-verified at Speed 1×: 50 Hz now traces 204, 223, 238, 249, 255, 257, 255, 249, 238, 223 — a resolved sinusoid; the Speed control still multiplies dt on top and never changes an answer
- FIXED [P1][FUNC/D8] **the light theme painted a black canvas and then wrote dark text on it.** `readColour` read `getComputedStyle(document.documentElement)` while the Shell's toggle puts `.light-theme` on `document.body` (:1829), so the canvas kept `--surface: rgba(6,14,17,.95)` under a light page — measured corner pixel (6, 13, 16) with `body --surface` = rgba(248,250,252,.96) — and the DOM plot-title and legend, which sit *on* the canvas, flipped to the light theme's `#334155` ink and became unreadable. Switched to `document.body`, added an `isLightTheme()`-driven `PALETTE` for the twelve hardcoded canvas colours (string, components, nodes, antinode guides, shaker, both status chips) and light overrides for the legend pill and its swatches (:2108–2130, :1116–1124) · re-verified: corner pixel (229, 231, 235), full screenshot legible at n = 1 and n = 3
- FIXED [P1][D8] **the words "node" and "antinode" appeared nowhere on screen, and neither did the glyphs that mean them.** The amber dots, the faint dashed verticals (α = 0.25), the grey clamp bars and the teal driver dot were all unlabelled; the legend carried one "components" entry for two visually distinct blue curves, so card 4's "one moving right, one moving left" could not be matched to anything drawn. Added in-scene callouts "node (never moves)" on the first interior node (the clamped end itself in the fundamental, where there is no interior node) and "antinode (swings most)" on the first antinode, both centre-clamped so they survive n = 20; "fixed end" over each clamp and "shaker" under the driver track; the guides raised to α = 0.45; the legend rebuilt as three line swatches — solid "the string", solid "wave sent in by the shaker", **dashed** "wave reflected back" — so the two components differ by dash as well as by label (:2236–2243, :2259–2266, :2292–2312, :1107–1111, :121–129)
- FIXED [P1][D8/DEMO] **the causal chain T, μ → v → λ → f was invisible: v and λ were never displayed anywhere.** `v = √(T/μ)` drove every number correctly but the student saw tension and mass-per-length change the frequency by magic. The Readouts box now reads down the chain — `Wave speed / v = √(T/μ)` → 100.0 m/s, `Nearest mode / λₙ = 2L/n` → λ₃ = 0.667 m, `Its frequency / fₙ = v/λₙ` → f₃ = 150.0 Hz, then `Locked to it?` — with the three numbers arithmetically consistent on screen at every grid point (:1319–1338, :2417–2421). The Controls-Guide step text was updated to match the new panel (:2738)
- FIXED [P1][LAYER] **two card claims were contradicted by the pixels, and the deck opened with UI choreography.** Card 1 said "the string writhes with no clean shape — no point stays still"; measured, the default state was a near-static single arch. Card 4 said the components are drawn "below the pink string"; they are drawn overlaid on it, crossing it. Cards rewritten to the SME grammar in `guided-inquiry/references/golden-flows.md` — a 3-sentence FRAME defining L, T, μ and v with no UI prose; Q1 as the naive baseline ("It grows steadily as the frequency rises" / "It is large only at a few separate frequencies" / "It is the same at every frequency"), which is answerable from swing intuition alone and is exactly what the new resonance response now shows; the harmonic-spacing and √T predicts kept with neutral stems and terse claim-only choices; card 4 demoted to a naming TELL that describes the solid and dashed curves correctly and defines *node* and *antinode*; "Press ▶ Play", "hit Next", "Sweep the frequency and watch" removed throughout. 6 cards, all feedback resolves on the spot. Control names in card copy aligned to the on-screen slider labels ("drive frequency", not "shaker frequency")
- FIXED [P1][LAYER] **card 2's predict gate leaked its answer from the boot screen.** The Readouts panel announced "f₁ = 50.0 Hz" and the off-resonance chip quoted "Δf = −20.0 Hz from f₁ = 50.0 Hz" while the card asked whether the string answers every frequency or only a few. Both staged by step: the panel and the chip's numeric clause appear from card 3 onward, and are fully present in Controls-Guide and Free-Exploration modes (:2106–2112, :2351–2353, :2489–2491) · verified live: cards 1–2 → `readoutsVisible false`, chip reads "off resonance — the string barely responds" with no frequency; card 3 → `true`; Reset → back to card 1 with the panel hidden
- FIXED [P2][DEMO] **the wave was capped at 58 % of the height available to it.** `ampPx = min((h − 120)/2 × 0.85, 180)` clamped at 180 px where 276 px was free at 1500×950, against the sim's own stated design principle "K1 — fill the frame by scaling". Cap raised to 260 (the 1024×768 case is height-limited at 183 and unchanged) (:2168)
- FIXED [P2][DEMO/D8] **the canvas grew faint ghost copies of the string.** The per-frame background fill used `--surface`, which is 95 % alpha, so 5 % of each frame survived into the next and the scene accumulated three or four unlabelled extra curves — real extraneous load for a reader still learning which curve is which. Switched to the opaque `--panel-bg` (:2142–2146)
- FIXED [P2][D8] **canvas text below the 12 px floor, a plural bug, and a missing unit.** The `x = 0` / `x = L = 1 m` axis labels were 11 px (→ 12 px); the locked chip read "1 loops, 2 nodes" at the fundamental (→ "1 loop"); the μ slider's minimum range label read a bare "0.0025" (→ "0.0025 kg/m"). No `ctx.font` site uses `var()` — all resolve through `readColour`, so none silently renders at 10 px
- ENHANCEMENTS (not applied, ROI-ranked): (1) **a string-length slider, and with it Mersenne's first law.** L is hardcoded at 1.0 m, so `f ∝ 1/L` — the third of the three laws the topic is built on, and the one behind every sonometer and every fretted-string question — cannot be demonstrated at any setting, while `f ∝ √T` and `f ∝ 1/√μ` both can. Sketch: `const L = 1.0` becomes `state.L` with an `#length` slider over 0.4–2.0 m, step 0.05; `physicsFor`, `stringShape`, `xToPx` and the audit's `lengthMetres` already take L as a parameter, so the change is the control plus `L → state.L` at six call sites; the right clamp moves with L against a fixed metres-to-pixels scale (rather than the present always-full-width mapping) so that shortening the string visibly *shortens* it instead of rescaling the picture — which is the whole point, and is also what makes "half the length, twice the frequency" a seen fact rather than a read number. (2) **a harmonic-series ladder under the string** — a horizontal frequency axis from 0 to 250 Hz with a tick at every f_n, a moving marker for the drive, and a resonance-response curve drawn over it; the student would *see* the comb of allowed frequencies and the drive sliding between the teeth, which is the mental image the topic needs and which the sim currently asks them to assemble from a chip that only speaks when they land on a tooth. (3) **a mode-comparison overlay** — a ghost of the n and n+1 patterns together, so "the same string, one more loop, exactly one more f₁" is a visible ratio rather than two remembered screens. (4) **a boundary-condition switch (one end free)** — the sibling C135's odd-only series, side by side with this one, is the cleanest way to show that the *boundary condition*, not the medium, chooses which harmonics exist. (5) **draw the transverse particle velocity** ∂y/∂t as short arrows at a few points, making "every point in a loop moves in phase, adjacent loops in antiphase" watchable; the engine already returns `particleVelocity`.
- Residual (accepted, not defects): at the extreme μ = 0.0025 kg/m with T = 400 N the fundamental is 200 Hz and only n = 1 fits under the 250 Hz slider ceiling — an honest consequence of the ranges, and every other corner of the grid reaches n ≥ 2; the lock window is a fixed 0.6 Hz in absolute terms, so it is relatively wider at f₁ = 12.5 Hz than at f₁ = 200 Hz, which is what a fixed damping width does; at n = 20 the "node (never moves)" callout is clamped inside the canvas and overlaps the string near the left clamp, unavoidable without a leader line; the only console entry at any grid point is the static server's `/favicon.ico` 404. Note for the committer: the inner `Final_JEE_sims` repo's HEAD predates the feedback-pill rollout, so `git diff` for this file carries 67 lines of pill markup that are **not** from this loop.
- Gates: syntax ✓ (7/7 inline scripts) · jsdom ×3 ✓ (welcome-overlay, guided-inquiry 6 cards, controls-tutorial 10 steps — all with `--baseline` from `git show HEAD:…`) · user-version-eval ✓ · layout-probe ✓ · canvas-check ✓ (`scene` backing:display 1.000/1.000 in both the pre-fix baseline and the patched build, zero drift) · copyright ✓
- S3: re-push required for this file.

## 2026-09-11 · `Module-08-Oscillations-and-Waves/C136-beats.html` — jee-sim-deep-review (Track P + Track D incl. D8 + Track L)

- Track P: grid **45 live points** (f₁ ∈ {200, 256, 440, 442, 500} × f₂ ∈ {200, 260, 440, 440.5, 441, 442, 444, 450, 500} — every slider extreme, unison Δf = 0, the two step-limited near-unison points 0.5/1.0 Hz, the equal-Δf/different-pitch pair 440-444 vs 256-260, and the widest Δf = 300 Hz), **0 mismatches** before and after the fixes against an independent Node oracle (f_beat = |f₁−f₂|, T = 1/|f₁−f₂|, carrier = ½(f₁+f₂), envelope = 2|cos(πΔf t)|; the oracle self-checks that |sum| never exceeds the envelope — worst excess 3e-12 over 400 k samples — and that the envelope's maxima are spaced 1/|Δf|, not 2/|Δf|). `__audit.at` agrees to 1e-12 at every point. **The classic beats error is NOT present**: the drawn envelope was fitted column-by-column against the oracle at five (f₁, f₂, t) states — **RMS 1.0 px, worst 1.9 px** on a 2 px stroke — while the 2/|Δf| impostor curve fits at RMS 24–43 px. Browser-verified yes (CDP :12156, 1500×950 and 1024×768, both themes).
- Track D report card: V 3 → 4 · C 3 → 4 · R 2 → 4 · L 2 → 4 · G 3 → 4 · **N (D8) 2 → 4**
- FIXED [P1][DEMO] **every Δf below 2 Hz — the entire piano-tuner regime cards 3, 4 and 6 are built on — drew a flat line while the readout announced a beat.** The time window had a cliff: `displayBeatPeriod > 30 → span = 6.0 s`. Measured cycles-in-window: Δf = 2 → 1.00, Δf = 1 → **0.10**, Δf = 0.5 → **0.05**, so at f₂ = 441 Hz the envelope swung 1.90→2.00 (a 5 % ripple, visually flat) under a readout reading "1.00 Hz / 1.000 s". Worse, the window jumped 30 s → 6 s between Δf = 2.0 and 1.9, so stepping f₂ *down* zoomed the axis 5× and the bulge appeared to **narrow** — the opposite of the physics the card asserts. The branch is gone; `span = min(30, max(2.5, 2.4·T_display))` with unison → 30 (:2160–2165), which is monotone in T_beat and simply holds at the sampling ceiling. · verified: drawn envelope swing (0 = node, 2 = peak) across the window is now 1.97 at Δf ≥ 2, 1.87 at Δf = 1, 0.56 at Δf = 0.5, 0.00 at unison; walking f₂ 444 → 440 as card 4 instructs, the envelope at the window's left edge reads 2.01, 0.06, 2.01, 1.41, 0.04, 1.43, 2.01 — exactly 2|cos(πΔf/2)| at every step
- FIXED [P1][DEMO] **the time axis disagreed with every readout by a factor of 60.** Ticks were labelled in the sim's *stretched* display time ("−25.77 s") while the sidebar said the beat period was 0.250 s, so a student who measured two swells off the axis got 15 s and a beat frequency of 0.067 Hz. The only disclosure was a 10 px note. Ticks now carry **real seconds** (`t / TIME_SCALE`, with the decimal count derived from the real span) and the note reads "time in seconds, 0 = now · drawn 60× slower than real so single cycles stay visible" (:2288–2300). · verified: at the default the axis runs −0.433 → 0.067 s, so the two visible swells span 0.50 s ⇒ 4 Hz, landing exactly on the "4.00 Hz / 0.250 s" readout; at Δf = 40 the axis span is 0.060 s = 2.4 × 0.025 s
- FIXED [P1][DEMO] **the per-row amplitude autoscale cancelled the one thing the sum panel exists to show.** Tones were drawn at `yAmp = (rowH/2−8)/1` and the sum at `/2`, so amplitude 1 and amplitude 2 rendered at the *same* pixel height (94.5 px each at 1500×950) — two unit tones visibly adding to nothing bigger. Same failure class as `mag-cyclotron`'s auto-scaled B-slider. One shared ±2 scale on all three rows now (:2199–2204, and the tick block) · verified by screenshot: the tone traces are half the height of the sum's constructive peaks and reach zero together at each node
- FIXED [P1][FUNC] **moving a slider while paused left the picture on the previous frequencies.** `draw()` was reachable only from `onFrame`/`onReset`/`onResize`/`onStep`; with `cfg.restartOnParamChange` (the shell default) a slider gesture ends with `onReset()` painting the **default** 440/444 and then the chosen value restored through a bare `input` that only touches the readouts. Live trace: paused, f₂ 444 → 480, readout went to "40.00 Hz" with the canvas byte-identical. Both handlers now repaint (:2040–2053) · verified: same gesture now changes the canvas hash (1617253134 → 526729440) while `Shell.playing` is false
- FIXED [P1][FUNC/D8] **every canvas label rendered at 10 px.** All five `ctx.font` assignments used `var(--font-sans)` / `var(--font-nums)`; canvas cannot parse `var()` so the whole declaration is rejected and the context keeps `10px sans-serif` — confirmed live (`ctx.font='600 11px var(--font-sans, sans-serif)'` → reads back `'10px sans-serif'`). The three row labels, both axis tick sets, the axis note and the beat-rate chip were all affected, i.e. every word drawn on the plot. Literal stacks now at 13 px / 12 px, above the D8 floor (:2100–2103 and the five call sites) · verified: the three fonts read back as `600 13px DMSansUser…`, `12px DMSansUser…`, `12px JetBrainsMonoNums…`
- FIXED [P1][D8] **the row labels were drawn in the trace's own colour directly on top of the trace**, which at this carrier density is a solid band — "Tone 1: f₁ = 440 Hz" was unreadable at both 1500×950 and 1024×768 (screenshot evidence). Each label now sits on a theme-coloured plate, the same device the beat chip already used, and the labels were reworded for a first encounter: "Tone 1 alone: f₁ = …", "Sum: Tone 1 + Tone 2, with its envelope" (:2178–2190, :2151–2153). The beat chip became "beat frequency |f₁ − f₂| = 4.00 Hz" (was a bare "beat") and drops to the row's bottom-right if a narrow canvas would let the two meet (:2358–2363) · verified: clearance between the two is 108 px at 1024×768 and 584 px at 1500×950 at every f₁/f₂ extreme
- FIXED [P1][LAYER] **card 4 told the student to read a pitch readout that did not exist** ("only the beat frequency and beat period update meaningfully; the pitch you hear (the average) barely moves"), and card 5's whole contrast — 440/444 against 256/260, same beat, different pitch — had no number anywhere on screen. Added the third readout `Pitch you hear ½(f₁ + f₂)` (:1183, :2033, :2063–2066), which the Formal panel already defined; card 4's copy now names what actually happens (:1113). The Controls-Guide step for that box was updated to match (:2668) · verified: walking f₂ 444 → 440, pitch reads 442.0 → 440.0 while the beat readout runs 4.00 → 0.00 Hz
- FIXED [P1][LAYER] **card 1 asked for an observation that cannot happen.** "Press ▶ and count" — the trace is drawn 60× slowed, so ten seconds of watching yields **0.67** throbs, while card 2 immediately answers "40 in ten seconds". It was also UI-choreography prose, which the SME grammar in `guided-inquiry/references/golden-flows.md` forbids. Replaced with the slowdown fact and the axis's units (:1088); the card holds the 90-word pre-answer budget at 86
- FIXED [P2][FUNC/D8] **the light theme never reached the canvas.** `cssVar()` read `document.documentElement` while the shell's toggle puts `light-theme` on `document.body` (shell :1769) — measured: `documentElement --surface` = `rgba(6,14,17,.95)` vs `body --surface` = `rgba(248,250,252,.96)`, canvas corner pixel unchanged at `[6,14,17]` after the toggle. Fixing the read alone would have shipped dark text on the hard-coded `rgba(6,14,17,.65)` label plate, so the plate and the four trace inks gained light variants (`#1d4ed8 / #be185d / #b45309 / #047857`) and the legend swatches follow them through CSS classes rather than inline styles (:91–98, :1070–1073, :2086–2126) · verified: canvas corner `[248,250,252]` after the toggle, `.ld-sum` computes to `rgb(180,83,9)`, both themes legible at both viewports
- FIXED [P2][LAYER] **the Info modal made two claims this build does not honour.** "K1 · the time axis rescales with the beat period so at least two throbs are always visible, even near unison" was false below Δf = 4 (and catastrophically so below 2 — see the first fix); "D6/D7 · the envelope curve appears when it is named; the meter arrives with its step" describes staging that does not exist — `onStep` is a deliberate no-op ("the drawing already contains everything", :2399) and the envelope's step guard `__stepIndex >= 0` is true at every step. Both cells now describe the build (:1031–1033), as does C1 now that there are three readouts
- FIXED [P2][D8] **the "Loudness meter" drew six identical ramp glyphs** (`▇▇▇▇▇▇`) that all changed together, which reads as a waveform rather than a level. Now a ten-cell filled/empty bar (`████░░░░░░`) under the label "Loudness right now" (:2338–2344, :1187). The legend gained word-glosses ("Sum of the two", "Envelope (loudness outline)") and the readout labels carry their symbol on a second line (`.stat-row.has-sub`, :122–123) — which also fixed a wrap the longer labels had introduced: `300.00 Hz` and `∞ — no beats` were breaking onto two lines at the slider extremes, and now measure 29 px and 22 px tall, one line each
- ENHANCEMENTS (not applied, ROI-ranked): (1) **an envelope-only view below ~2 Hz, and real audio.** Two beat cycles at Δf = 1 Hz contain 880 carrier cycles — physically undrawable as a polyline on a 1150 px canvas, which is why the 30 s ceiling exists and why Δf = 1 still shows only half a cycle. Sketch: when `f_avg/Δf` exceeds ~300 carrier cycles per window, stop stroking the sum and fill the band between ±2|cos(πΔf t)| instead (the envelope arrays are already computed), so the window can open to 2.4 beat periods at *any* Δf; the tone rows stay on the current ceiling with a "carrier not resolvable at this zoom" note. Pair it with a `WebAudio` two-oscillator source gated behind a Sound button — beats are an *auditory* phenomenon and this sim, whose first card is titled "Look and listen (with your eyes)", is silent. (2) **a phase-drift strip** — the *mechanism* (the two tones sliding from in-phase to anti-phase and back once per beat period) is never drawn; two rotating phasors, or a Δφ(t) = 2πΔf·t ramp under the traces, would turn "the envelope swells" from a result into a cause. (3) **a second, locked reference pair** — card 5's claim (440/444 and 256/260 beat identically) is currently answered from memory across two slider moves; a ghost envelope from a frozen second pair would make the ratio visible in one frame. (4) **unequal amplitudes** — with A₁ ≠ A₂ the nodes stop reaching zero (y_min = |A₁−A₂|), the standard JEE follow-up; both amplitudes are hard-coded to 1 and `superposeWaves` already takes them per component. (5) **mark the audibility limit** — beats are only *heard* below Δf ≈ 10–15 Hz, but the sliders reach Δf = 300 Hz and the sim calls it a beat throughout; a shaded band on the f₂ slider plus one line in the Formal panel would keep the regime honest.
- Residual (accepted, not defects): the transport is slow by design — one throb takes 15 wall-clock seconds at the default, because `TIME_SCALE = 60` buys the resolvable carrier; the static trace already shows two full beats at boot, so the aha does not depend on waiting, and 4× Speed is available (verified: Δt = 1.50 vs 6.00 display-s over the same 1.5 s, with every readout unchanged — speed is playback only). The beat-frequency readout is visible at card 2's gate, judged *not* a leak: the card asks for throbs in **ten seconds** and the "About 4" distractor exists precisely to punish reading the rate without multiplying. Both sliders span 200–500 Hz, so Δf reaches 300 Hz, far outside the beats regime — the superposition drawn there is still exact (grid-verified) and enhancement 5 covers the labelling. The only console entry at any grid point is the static server's `/favicon.ico` 404.
